@@99164521 <h> iProcure CEO Niraj Varia On Revamping Africa 's Agrifood Supply Chain <p> Despite its promising prospects , agritech in Africa is yet to punch above the weight of sectors like fintech and eCommerce , neither does sector have bragging rights to startups the caliber and worth of Fawry or Jumia . At present , it remains a market yet in need of specific solutions to its real-world problems . <p> Where technology branches into agriculture , the supply chain is rightfully the grayest area . A complex network existing between farmers and consumers has historically been constrained by fragmented logistics , low-to-no visibility , limited market access , and a paucity of transparency . <p> Climate change makes the situation even more dire . Early-stage ventures , in response , are digging deeper to help mitigate the crisis ' effects not only on the supply chains but also on the agritech industry at large . With food production hampered and feeding costs soaring , there is no better time than present for agritechs to break the dam . <p> Niraj Varia is the current CEO of Kenya 's iProcure , a self-styled " agtech " that connects agricultural @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ mid-2022 , Niraj was a partner at London-based Novastar Ventures , which has invested in TradeDepot , BasiGo , Moniepoint , Turaco , SolarNow , mPharma , and iProcure -- easily some of Africa 's fastest-growing tech firms . <p> Last August , iProcure , which was founded in 2014 , raised USD 10.2 M in Series B to scale its presence in Kenya and Uganda and expand into another East African market . In early May , the agritech launched operations in Tanzania and partnered with Farm to Market Alliance ( FtMA ) . <p> In an exclusive interview with WeeTracker , Niraj Varia posits that the African agritech industry can even the odds by focusing more on using actionable data to improve the agricultural supply chain , as it would better position ventures to innovate against the rather uncertain days ahead . <h> This interview has been edited for length and clarity . <h> Before iProcure , you were in VC . What are some valuable lessons you 've gained from your time in the industry that have been beneficial in your current work ? <p> Niraj : @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ venture capitalists . Understanding what investors want to hear and how to present it has made it easier for us at IProcure to raise funding and gain support . <p> Additionally , for over 8 years as a Partner at Novastar Ventures , I had the opportunity to evaluate thousands of companies . Although my assessments were relatively brief , they provided me with a broad perspective and valuable insights into what works , and what does n't , and also how to identify common patterns . <p> This extensive exposure has proven immensely valuable in my current role . For instance , my experiences serving on the boards of companies tackling complex data-sorting projects have taught me the inherent challenges involved . <p> Consequently , in my current role , I prioritize avoiding extensive investments of time in similar endeavors , ensuring our resources are utilized as efficiently and as effectively as possible . <p> Moreover , observing companies expand into new countries and geographies has provided me with invaluable insights into successful strategies , pitfalls to avoid , and the misleading allure of certain opportunities . <h> What @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ can data-centric tech lend to overhauling them ? <p> Niraj : While there are many promising agritech startups in Africa , much more emphasis should be placed on these ventures ensuring that farmers can access input as and when needed . <p> Production practices , off-take , training , and financing , among others , are important components of the ecosystem , of course . We , however , are focused on helping farmers struggling to obtain inputs due to a lack of data visibility within the supply chain . <p> In East Africa , for example , farmers typically purchase agricultural inputs , such as fertilizers and seeds , from small-scale retail shops called agro-dealers . However , these retailers lack visibility into their supply chains , often relying on pen and paper systems , and experiencing issues like stock discrepancies and pilferage . <p> This lack of data extends across the entire value chain , making it difficult for manufacturers , distributors , and retailers to make informed decisions . As a result , farmers frequently face difficulties in accessing the right inputs when they need them . @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ one of the longest-operating companies in the space , what has iProcure contributed to breaking the ice ? <p> Niraj : We are digitizing the supply chain by providing retailers with a mobile-based retail ERP system , one which includes point-of-sale , inventory management , order processing , purchasing , CRM , and business analytics capabilities . The system empowers them to operate more efficiently . <p> By digitizing the supply chain , there is full visibility into retailers ' sales data , enabling stakeholders to inform and improve operations , both for our own business as well as those of our suppliers . It is possible to track agro produce from manufacturer to storefront . <p> The data collected is used to credit score retailers and provide them inputs on credit . Last year , we extended credit to 1,500 retailers in Kenya . The retailers receive inputs with zero working capital cost , leading to increased sales . Our credit repayment rate stands at a high 99% with fast repayment cycles . <h> Could you give insights into how much visibility the availability of said data can bring @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ from the manufacturer to the aggregator , through warehousing , logistics , and deliveries . By fully digitizing the supply chain , we have achieved a remarkable 96% on-time fulfillment rate , significantly improving efficiency in rural Africa . <p> Our data-first approach to disrupting the agricultural input supply chain enables us to provide retailers with the tools they need to operate professionally and gain visibility into their operations . <p> Through the same process , we are actively working to address climate change concerns by focusing on the use of appropriate products and exploring the introduction of organic fertilizers to reduce the reliance on chemical alternatives . <h> Artificial intelligence ( AI ) is the new fish in tech . Does it have a place in your efforts in agritech ? <p> Niraj : We have several initiatives underway in terms of exploring advanced technologies in our operations . While we may not develop these technologies internally , we are increasingly collaborating with partners , some of whom enable us to harness the power of AI . <p> One such example is our partnership with a company that utilizes @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ our warehouses . By seamlessly integrating their technology into our systems , we have significantly enhanced our ability to expedite stock movement . <p> This operational transformation is made possible by the invaluable support of numerous solutions providers who are actively assisting in optimizing processes .
